1b1(mdo sde): @##//rgya gar skad du/Ar+ya ma hA 
1b2(mdo sde): sarba nI pA ta rat+na ke/tu hA ra Ni nI 
1b3(mdo sde): ma ma hA A na sU tra/bod skad du/'phags 
2a1(mdo sde): @##//pa 'dus pa chen po rin po che tog gi gzungs 
2a2(mdo sde): shes pa ba the ga pa chen po'i mdo//de bzhin gshegs 
2a3(mdo sde): pa 'od zho spos snang dpal la phyag 'tshal lo//tshangs pa 
2a4(mdo sde): chen po dbyangs can la phyag 'tshal lo//de la phyag 'tshal 
2b1(mdo sde): te bdud kyi 'khor gyis mi tshugs pa zhes bya ba'i gzungs 'di sbyor bar 
2b2(mdo sde): bgyis bdag gi rig sngags 'di grub par gyur cig//o 'di
2b3(mdo sde): la e A bA me/A bA me/A ba rai/A ba re/pa
2b4(mdo sde): ri kuny+dza /nang dA da/puska ra bA ha/dzA lu khA/ma khA ya/
2b5(mdo sde): i 'a mi li/ki li mi li/kI ra di pa rA mu dro/mu dre/mu kha 
3a1(mdo sde): @##//swAhA|oM////'di skad bdag gis thos pa dus gcig na/btsom ldane 
3a2(mdo sde): 'das rgyal po'i khab 'od ma'i tshal ba ka lan da ka gnas pa na/dge slong stong du tshang 
3a3(mdo sde): ba'i dge slong gi dge 'adun chen po thams cad kyang dag bcom pa/zag 
3a4(mdo sde): pa zad pa/nyon mongs pa med pa/dbang dang ldan par gyur pa/sems shin tu 
3a5(mdo sde): rnam par grol ba/shes rab shin tu rnam par grol ba/cang shes pa/glang 
3b1(mdo sde): po chen po//bya ba byas pa/byed pa byas pa/khur bor ba/bdag gi don rab tu thob pa/srid par kun 
3b2(mdo sde): tu sbyor ba yongs su zad pa/yang dag pa'i shes pas/sems shin tu rnam par grol ba/shes rab shin 
3b3(mdo sde): tu rnam par grol ba/sems kyi dbang thams cad kyi dam pa'i pha rol tu/son pa sha stag dang thabs 
3b4(mdo sde): gcig tu bzhugs so//byang chub sems dpa' khri'i byang chub sems dpa'i dge 'dun 
3b5(mdo sde): chen po 'di lta ste/lhun po'i rtsa 'dzin blo gros gzhon nur gyur pa dang /chu lha'i blo gros gzhon nur 
3b6(mdo sde): gyur pa dang /rgyal po'i blo gros gzhon nur gyur ba dang /'od kyi blo gros gzhon nur gyurab pa dang /
4a1(mdo sde): nam mkha'i blo gros gzhon nur gyur pa dang /glog gi blo gros gzhon nur gyur ba dang /'jam 
4a2(mdo sde): dpal gzhon nur gyur pa dang thub dka' gzhon nur gyur pa dang/chu lha gzhon nur gyur pa dang dri ma med pa 
4a3(mdo sde): gzhon nur gyur pa dang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po byams pa dang /de dag la sogs pa byang 
4a4(mdo sde): chub sems dpa' khri thams cad bzod pa dang /gzungs dang /ting nge 'dzin thob pa/chos thams cad 
4a5(mdo sde): la sgrib pa med pa'i ye shes can/sems can thams cad la sems mnyam pa/bdud kyi yul 
4a6(mdo sde): thams cad las yang dag par 'das pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi yul la 'jug pa shes pa la 
4a7(mdo sde): mkhas pa/byams pa chen po dang /snying rje chen po dang ldan pa/thabs shes pa la mkhas pa sha stag dang thabs 
4b1(mdo sde): gcig tu bzhugs so//de'i tshe rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen po na/kun tu rgyu mkhas pa/gsal ba/yid gzhungs pa/rigs pa'i gnas 
4b2(mdo sde): bco brgyad kyi pha rol tu phyin pa/g.yog lnga brgya pa gcig gi ming ni nye rgyal/gcig gis ming ni pang nas skyes shes bya ba gnyis shig 'khod de/
4b3(mdo sde): tshogs kyi gtso bo//g.yog gi tshogs can de gnyis phan tshun 'di ltar bdag cag las gang gis thog mar bdud rtsi thob pa des gcig la gcig 
4b4(mdo sde): sbran to zhes dam tshig byas so//de nas tshe dang ldan pa rta thul snga dro'i dus kyi tshe/sham thab/sa dang /chos gos bgos lhung bzed thogs 
4b5(mdo sde): nas/rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyerachen por bsod snyoms kyi phyir zhugs pa dang /kun tu rgyu nye rgyal gyis/tshe dang ldan pa rta thul spyod yul du 
4b6(mdo sde): zhugs nas/rgyal po'i khab kyi grongs khyer chen por bsod snyoms kyi phir song ba mthong ngo //mthong nas de 'di snyam du sems te/ji ltar 
4b7(mdo sde): dge slong 'di'i spyod lam mdzes pa de lta bu ni/bdag gis sngan chad dge sbyong ngam/bram ze 'am/mir gyur pa gzhan su la yang gang yang sngon 
4b8(mdo sde): ma mthong na/bdag de'i gan du song ste/tshe dang ldan pa khyod kyi ston pa gang yin/gang las rab tu byung /gang gi chos la dga' 
5a1(mdo sde): @##//zhes dri'o snyam nas/de nas kun tu rgyu nye rgyal/tshe dang ldan pa rta thul ga la ba der song ste phyin nas/tshe dang ldan pa rta 
5a2(mdo sde): thul dang lhan cig mngon du yang dag par dga' bar bya ba dang /yang dag par mgu bar bya ba'i gtam sna tshogs byas te/phyogs gcig 
5a3(mdo sde): tu 'dug go/phyogs gcig tu 'dug nas/kun tu rgyu nye rgyal gyis/tshe dang ldan pa rta thul la 'di skad ces smras so//tshe 
5a4(mdo sde): dang ldan pa khyod kyi ston pa gang /gang las rab tu byung /ga las chos la dga'/de nas tshe dang ldan pa rta thul gyis/
5a5(mdo sde): kun tu rgyu nye rgyal la 'di skad ces smras /so//shAkya'i sras po brtul zhugs dka' thub chen po kun gyis dam pa 
5a6(mdo sde): dbang ldan pa/'khor ba'i rgya mtsho pha rol phyin cing grol la de bzhin 'gro ba 'grol mdzad pa/sangs rgyas shes bya 
5a7(mdo sde): sad mdzad mi mnyam sdug bsngal mtsho skems da ltar 'di na yod/dri ma med pa de'i skyabs su nga song nga ni de'i chos la dga'/nye rgyal 
5a8(mdo sde): gyis smras pa/khyod kyi ston pa ji skad smra/ji skad ston/tshe dang ldan pa rta thul gyi smras pa/de'i phyir tshe dang ldan pa legs 
5b1(mdo sde): par rab tu nyon la yid la zung shig dang /nges bshad do//ji ltar 'jig rten las dang nyon mongs rgyur bcas byed rgyur ldan 'byung dang /las 
5b2(mdo sde): dang nyon mongs pa dag ldog rgyu de yang 'dren pas rab tu gsungs/gang na skye dang rga dang rgud pa'i sdug bsngal nges par mi gnas pa/thar ba'i 
5b3(mdo sde): mchog de smra ba'i khyu mchog de yis rang gis mkhyen te gsungs/de nas kun tu rgyu nye rgyal gyis/chos kyi rnam grangs 'di thos 
5b4(mdo sde): nas/chos rnams la chos kyi mig rdul med cing dri ma bral la/nyon mongs pa med pa skyes te/des rgyun tu zhugs 
5b5(mdo sde): pa'i 'bras bu thob nas/de'i tshe tshig sa su bcad pa 'di skad ces smras so//sangs rgyas kyis ni shin tu rnyed dka' thob 
5b6(mdo sde): gter bdud rtsi'i chos bshad pa/'di ni rtag tu skye ba'i 'bab chu skems par bden pa khong du chud/de ni 'gro ba rnams kyi 
5b7(mdo sde): sdug bsngal rab tu zhi bar 'gyur ba ste/lam 'di dam pa shes rab chos rnams sgom pa mi mnyam byed pa yin/
5b8(mdo sde): de nas kun tu rgyu nye rgyal kyis/tshe dang ldan pa rta thul la 'di skad ces smras so/tshe dang ldan pa de bzhin gshegs pa dgra 
6a1(mdo sde): bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas ded lta ga la bzhugs tshe dang ldan pa rta thul gyis smras pa//tshe dang ldan pa bcom ldan 'das ni rgyal po'i 
6a2(mdo sde): khab 'od ma'i tshal bya ka lan da ka gnas pa na//'di lta ste/ral pa can stong rab tu byung ba'i dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang thabs gcig tu bzhugaso//nye rgyal gyis smras 
6a3(mdo sde): pa/bdag gis grogs po'i mchog dang slob ma'i 'khor la bltas te/bcom ldan 'das kyis thad du rab tu 'byung ngo //de nas kun tu rgyu nye rgyal/tshe dang ldan pa rta thul gyi rkang pa 
6a4(mdo sde): la mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas/lan gsum bskor ba byas te slar song nas/kun tu rgyu pang nas skyes ga la ba der song ba dang /kun tu rgyu pang nas skyed kyis/kun tu rgyu 
6a5(mdo sde): nye rgyal rgyang ring po nas 'ong pa mthong ste/mthong nas kun tu rgyu nye rgyal la 'di skad ces smraso//tshe dang ldan pa khyod kyi dbang po rnams ni dangs/bzhin gyi mdangs ni yongs 
6a6(mdo sde): su dag/lpags pa'i mdog ni kun tu dkar ba las na/tshe dang ldan pa khyod kyis bdud rtsi rnyed do//nye rgyal gyis smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa de de bzhin te/kho bos bdud rtsi rnyed kyis/tshe dang 
6a7(mdo sde): ldan pa de'i phyir legs par rab tu nyon la yid la zung shig dang /kho bos ci snyed pa khyod la bshad do//de nas kun tu rgyu pang nas skyes stan las langs nas/bla gos phrag pa cig 
6a8(mdo sde): tu bzar nas/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs nas/kun tu rgyu nye rgyal ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud de/'di skad ces smras so//gang gis srid gsum rgya 
6b1(mdo sde): mtsho myur rgal zhing /phung po dgra chen rnam par gzhig bya ba//mya ngan med zhi lam de shod cig dang //yang 'byung med pa'i lam du 'jug par bya//de nas kun tu rgyu nye rgyal gyis 'di skad 
6b2(mdo sde): ces smraso//ji ltar 'jig rten las dang nyon mongs rgyur bcas byed rgyur ldan 'byung dang //las dang nyon mongs pa dag ldog rgyu de yang 'dren pas rab tu gsungs/gang na skye dang rga dang rgud 
6b3(mdo sde): pa'i sdug bsngal nges par mi gnas pa//thar pa'i mchog de smra ba'i khyu mchog de yis rang gis mkhyen te gsungs//pang nas skyes kyis smras pa/sdug bsngal rab tu zhi bar bya phyir 
6b4(mdo sde): dri ma med pa zhi ba'i chos//nyon mongs lta ngan nyes pa thams cad zhi byed mi shes gcod pa dang //'di ni 'du su byas gsog gsob de phyir stong pa slu bar thub pas gsungs//de thos zhi ba 
6b5(mdo sde): reg 'gyur dri ma med pa'i tshig de zlos te gsungs/nye rgyal gyis smras pa//ji ltar 'jig rten la dang nyon mongs rgyur bcad byed rgyur ldan 'byung dang//las dang nyon mongs
6b6(mdo sde): pa dag ldog rgyu de yang bden pas rab tu gsungs//gang na skye dang rga dang rgung pa'i sdug bsngal nges par ma gnas pa//mthar pa mchog de smra ba'i khyu mtshog de yis rang gis mkhyenate 
6b7(mdo sde): gsungs//de nas kun tu rgyu dang nas skyes kyis de nyid du chos rnams la chos kyi mig rdul med cing ma dang bral la/nyon mongs pa med pa rnaMr sbyangs te/des rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras 
6b8(mdo sde): bu thob nas 'di skad ces smras so//yang dag spyod pa 'di ni chu bo sgrol ba zhi ba mgyogs pa'i gzings//ye shes mchog 'di sdug bsngal gsum po zhi byed 'khor ba'i pha rol 'gro/'di 
7a1(mdo sde): na yongs su shes na nyon mongs phung po 'joms zhing bdud 'dul 'gyur//thar pa 'di ni dgra dang thab mo bsal cing sdug bsngal rgya mtsho skems//bang 
7a2(mdo sde): nas skyes kyis smras pa/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas de da lta ga la bzhugs/nye rgyal gyis smras pa/tshe dang 
7a3(mdo sde): ldan pa bdag gis bcom ldan 'das rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen po 'od ma'i tshal bya ka lan da ka gnas pa na/dge slong gi dge 'dun chen po dang /byang chub sems dpa'i 
7a4(mdo sde): dge 'dun chen po dang thabs gcig tu bzhugs shes thos na/bdag cag gnyis bcom ldan 'das de'i thad du ra ba tu 'byung ngam//pang nas skyes kyis smras pa/tshe dang 
7a5(mdo sde): ldan ba de bzhin te bdag bdag gi 'khor la bltas la rab tu 'byungo//de nas kun tu rgyu nye rgyal dang /pang nas skyes rang gi 'khor ga la ba dong ngo//de nas skad cig 
7a6(mdo sde): thang cig yud tsam de la/bdud sdig can gyis yul a g+ha ma g+ha d+ha na mkhas shing snyan pa dang /brjod pa dang /grags par ldan pa/skyes bu dam pa nye rgyal dang /pang nas 
7a7(mdo sde): g.yog dang bcas te/dge sbyong go'u ta ma'i bstan pa la rab tu 'byung bar 'dod do zhes thos nas/de 'di snyam du sems so//kyi hud de gnyis gal te dge sbyong go'u ta ma'i slob 
7a8(mdo sde): mar gyur na/bdag gi bdud kyi yul stongs par byed kyis bdag der song ste/skyes bu dam pa de gnyis rab tu byung ba las bzlog la/sdig pa can gyi lta ba 'dzin du gzhugo snyam 
7b1(mdo sde): ste/de nas skad cig thang cig yud tsam la bdud sdig can myur du bdag gi khang pa nas mi snang bar byas te/tshe dang ldan pa rta thul gyi cha byad dang /rtags dang /spyod lam du bsgyur nas/
7b2(mdo sde): skyes bu dam pa de gnyis kyi mdun na lam kar 'dug ste/'di skad ces smras so//kho bos sngar smras rgyu dang byed rgyu lta bu de ni log pa ste khyed gnyis yid kyi spyod pa ci la nges 
7b3(mdo sde): pa bgam par bya ba'i phyir//smras pa de dag thams cad don yod ma yin 'di la rgyu yang med//dge dang mi dge'i las de'i 'bras bu thob pa 'di na ga la yod//myur du khyed gnyis 
7b4(mdo sde): 'dod pa'i yon tan dag la rab spyod rtsed mo byos//skye rga na 'chi med cing sdug bsngal med de 'jig rten pha rol med//bsod nams bsod nams ma yin las bskyed 'bras med 
7b5(mdo sde): rgyu dang byed pa med/rnyed phyir 'dir ni shA kyi'i bu yis smras kyis dad pas ma 'gro shig/de nas nye rgyal dang /pang nas skyes 'di snyam du sems te/kye ma bdud 
7b6(mdo sde): sdig can bdag cag rab tu 'byung ba las bzlog pa'i phyir 'ong so snyam nas/de nas nye rgyab kyis phyogs te 'dug nas/rang gi 'khor la bos te 'di skad ces smraso//bram 
7b7(mdo sde): ze'i khye'u khyod nyon cig 'khor ba'i skyon/dran par gyis la 'jig rten rga ba yis//gzir cing 'chi bdag gis ni rab bskor ba//de gnyis spang phyir rang byung legs par 
7b8(mdo sde): zung de nas spang nas skyes kyis bdud la slar smras pa/mkhas pa'i blo 'chang sdug bsngal gsum mthar 'byin mchog chos rnams kun shes kyis//rnam pa kun tu bdag 
8a1(mdo sde): cag blo gros 'di las bskyod nus su yang med//srid pa rab zhi bya phyir bdag cag brtan pa'i yid kyis rtag tu 'bad//seng ge'i gzugs can wa 
8a2(mdo sde): yi tshig gis nged kyi blo gros ma sgyur cig/bden pa mthong ba'i lha gang dag nam mkha' la 'dug pa de dag gis skyes bu dam pa de gnyis legs so zhes bya ba byin te/skyes bu dam 
8a3(mdo sde): pa khyed legs so legs so//'di lta ste/rab tu 'byung ba dang /mngon par 'byung ba 'di ni 'jig rten thams cad las khyad par 'phags pa'i lam yin te/lam 'di ni 
8a4(mdo sde): sdug bsngal thams cad zhi ba'o//lam 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi spyod yul la 'jug pa'o//'di lta ste/rab tu byung ba dang /mngon par byung ba 
8a5(mdo sde): 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das thams cad kyis rab tu bsngags shing bstod pa'i lam mo//de nas yang bdud sdig can sdug bsngal zhing yid mi bde 
8a6(mdo sde): ste/yid la bcags nas de nyid du mi snang bar song ngo //de nas kun tu rgyu nye rgyal dang /pang nas skyes gnyis kyis rang gi 'khor la bltas te 'di skad ces smras so//bram ze'i 
8a7(mdo sde): khye'u dag nged ni rga ba dang 'chi ba'i rgya mtsho'i pha rol tu 'gro ba'i phyir/de bzhin gshegs pa las rab tu 'byung bar yang dag par chas kyis khyed kyis shes par gyis shig 
8a8(mdo sde): khyod rnams las gang bcom ldan 'das kyi bstan pa la rab tu 'byung mi 'dod pa de ni 'di nas phyi rol log shig/de nas bram ze'i khye'u lnga brgya po de dag thams cad 
8b1(mdo sde): kyis 'di skad ces smraso //bdag cag cung zad shes pa de kun kyang khyed kyi mthun //khyed rgya chen po'i gnas su rab tu 'byung na/khyed gang las rab tu byung ba de las bdag cag kyang 
8b2(mdo sde): rab tu 'byung ngo //de nas kun tu nye rgyal dang /pang nas skyes g.yog lnga brgya dang bcas /bcom ldan 'das las rab tu 'byung bar chas par bdud sdig can gyis rig nas /ji nas
8b3(mdo sde): de gnyis dge sbyong go'u ta ma'i gan du 'gro mi nus par bya ba'i phyir /rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen po'i phyi logasu g.yang sa chen po zabs su dpag tshad brgya pa zhig sprul to//de nas 
8b4(mdo sde): bac+dos kyis kyang 'di 'dra ba'i rdzu 'phrul mngon par 'du bya ba mngon par 'du mdzad do//ci nas kun tu rgyu nye rgyal dang /pang nasskyes gnyis g.yang sa chen po de mi mthong zhing lam 
8b5(mdo sde): drang por 'gro bar mdzado //yang bdud sdig can gyis de gnyis kyi 'dun du ri chen po brtan pa /sra ba/ma rnyil ba /su la med pa/gcig tu stug por gyur pa/mkhregs pa/
8b6(mdo sde): 'phang du dpag tshad stong yod pa zhig mngon par sprul te/seng ge khro zhing gdug la gtum pa /sgra chen po 'byin pa stong yang mngon par sprul nas/bcom ldan 'das kyis gzi brjid dang /
8b7(mdo sde): rdzu 'phrul gyi mthus skyes bu dam pa de gnyis kyis ri de yang ma mthong /seng ge yang ma mthong /seng ge'i skad kyang ma thos te/lam drang por bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logasu song ngo //
8b8(mdo sde): bcom ldan 'das ni 'khor mang po brgya stong du mas yong su bskor cing /mdun gyis bltas nas chos ston te/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis dge slong rnams la bka' stsal pa//dge slong 
9a1(mdo sde): dag khyed skyes bu dam pa tshogs kyi gtso bo g.yog gi tshogs can 'di gnyis la ltos/de dag gis gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das mthong la gso//bcom ldan 'das 
9a2(mdo sde): kyis bka' stsal pa/skyes bu dam pa 'di gnyis g.yog dang bcas te nga'i thad du rab tu byung nas/gcig ni nga'i nyan thos thams cad kyi nang na shes rab can gyi mchog tu 'gyur ro//gcig 
9a3(mdo sde): ni rdzu 'phrul can gyi mchog tu 'gyur ro //de nas dge slong zhig gis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//skyes bu mkhas pa g.yog ldan 'di gnyis ni//rdzu 'phrul ldan dang shes rab 
9a4(mdo sde): 'jigs med par//mi mchog phan pa mdzad pas lung bstan te//bdag gis de gnyis sti stang bskyed nas bsu//de nas dge slong de stan las langs nas/dge slong mang po 
9a5(mdo sde): khyim na gnas pa dang /rab tu byung ba mang po dang lhan cig tu skyes bu dam pa de gnyis bsu nas bsnyen bkur byas so//de nas skyes bu dam pa de gnyis pa bcom ldan 'das ga la ba 
9a6(mdo sde): der song ste phyin nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te/lan gsum bskor ba byas nas/bcom ldan 'das kyis spyan sngar 'dug ste/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad 
9a7(mdo sde): ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das rab tu 'byung ba dang /bsnyen par rdzogs pa dge slong gi dngos po bdag cag gnyis la stsal du gsol/bdag cag bcom ldan 'das kyi thad du tshungs 
9a8(mdo sde): par spyod pa spyad do//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu khyed gnyis kyi ming ci//nye rgyal gyis gsol pa/bdag ni bram ze nye rgyal gyi bu lags te/nye rgyal zhes bgyi/
9b1(mdo sde): bdag gi ma'i ming ni shA ri'i ka ste/bdag de las skyes pas na/de'i slad du bdag gi ming ni shA ri'i bu zhes btags so//bdag rab tu 'byung bar yang pha mas gnang lags so//pang nas 
9b2(mdo sde): skyes kyis gsol pa/bdag gis pha'i ming ni ko'u di n+ya zhes bgyi/bdag gi ma'i ming ni mo'u dgal zhes bgyi ste/bdag de las skyes bas na/de'i slad du bdag ga ming ni mo'u 
9b3(mdo sde): dgal gyi bu zhes btags so//ba dag rab tu 'byung bar ya nga pha mas gnang lags so //bcom ldan 'das kyis shA ri'i bu dang /mo'u dgal gyi bu nga'i thad du g.yog dang bcas te/tshangs 
9b4(mdo sde): par spyad pa spyod cig bka' stsal pa dang /de nyid kyis de gnyis rab tu byung ba dang /bsnyen par rdzogs shing dge slong gi dngos por gyur to//shA ri'i bu dang /mo'u gal gyi bu g.yog dang 
9b5(mdo sde): bcas pa rab tu byung nas ring po ma lon par bdud sdig can dbang phyug chen po'i gzugs su bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar 'dug nas/'di skad ces smras so//gang dag 
9b6(mdo sde): bstan bcos don spyod mkhas pa rig pa'i pha rol song /de kun nga'i rkang la phyag 'tshal de dag 'dren pa nga //go'u tam slob ma 'khor bcas nga la deng myur skyabs tshal cig/mya 
9b7(mdo sde): ngan 'das 'gro rgyas pa gsal ba'i lam 'di khyod la bshad//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/khyod lam 'gro ba 'di dag ngan 'gror 'dren cing sdug bsngal rgya mtsho rnyed 
9b8(mdo sde): par byed/nga yi lam ni rgyu dang mi rgyu ba yi 'gro ba'i sdug bsngal rgya mtsho skems byed pa//'chal pa 'dzem pa med cing mu cor smra ba wa skad da yang ci zhig zer/khyod ni bcom 
10a1(mdo sde): zin bdud kyis nga la da yang 'dir ni gnod gang byed mi nus//de nas bdud sdig can gyis dbang phyug chen po'i gzugs mi snang bar byas nas/tshangs pa'i 
10a2(mdo sde): cha byad du bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar 'dug te/'di skad ces smras so//khyod kyis shes rab kyis ni las dang nyon mongs srid pa'i myu gu rab tu bsal//de slan chad thub 
10a3(mdo sde): pa ci slad sems can don 'dir 'di ltar sdug bsngal spro//gtso bo 'gro ba 'di na khyod kyi snod gyur skye bo gang na'ang ma mchis te//dus 'di la gas na skyon dang bral khyod ci'i 
10a4(mdo sde): slad du mya ngan myur mi 'da'//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa//ngas 'dus gang 'khod sems can mya ngan mtshungs med gang ga'i bya ma snyed//ngas mthong de dag nga yis snying 
10a5(mdo sde): rjes phra bar bya ba yin//'gro ba rab 'bring ngan par gyur pa thar te mthar thug na//de nas mya ngan 'da' yis blo gros ngan pa g.yos ci bskul//de nas yang bdud 
10a6(mdo sde): sdig can sdug bsngal zhing yid mi bde nas yid la bcags ste/de nyid du mi snang bar gyur nas rang gi gnas su song ste//mya ngan gyi khang par zhugs nas 'dug pa dang /
10a7(mdo sde): de'i mod la bdud kyi gnas na 'khod pa'i sems can kun phan tshun 'di skad du ci'i rgyus bdag cag gis rgyal po 'di mya ngan gyi khang par zhugs te 'dug pa su'ang mi shes 
10a8(mdo sde): so zhes 'dri'o//de nas bdud kyi bu mo lnga brgya tsam rab tu dga' bar byed pa'i me tog dang /bdug pa dang /spos dang /phreng ba dang /byug pa thogs te rab tu yid du 'ong 
10b1(mdo sde): ba'i gos dang /lhab lhub gyis bdag cag brgyan nas/rab tu yid du 'ong zhing myos par byed pa'i lha'i sil snyan byas te/rab tu yid du 'ong ba'i dbyangs kyis glu len gar byed 
10b2(mdo sde): cing phan lag lnga dang ldan pa'i lha'i sil snyan dag byas nas/dga' ba'i rtsed mo dang ldan par bdud sdig can gyi mdun 'khod pa dang /bdud sdig can des lag pa gnyis 
10b3(mdo sde): bsgreng nas bos te/sgra ma byed/sgra ma byed ces de skad smras pa dang /yud tsam zhig lha'i bu mo dag cang mi zer bar 'khod do//yang glu rab tu blangs shing sil snyan byas 
10b4(mdo sde): pa dang /bdud sdig canagyis lag pa gnyis g.yabs te/bos so//lha'i bu mo de dag gis dga' ba dang ldan pa'i glu dang gar dang /sil snyan lan bdun gyi bar du 
10b5(mdo sde): byas pa dang /yang de bzhin du bdud sdig can de lag pa gnyis bsgreng nas 'bod cing /sgra ma byed sgra ma byed ces zer de skad ces smras pa dang lha'i bu de dag cang mi 
10b6(mdo sde): zer bar 'khod de//de nas lha'i bu mo dbyangs snyan glog ces bya bas//bdud sdig can ga la ba de logasu thal mo sbyar bas btud nas 'di skad ces smras so/gtso bo 
10b7(mdo sde): khyod kyis deng 'dir shi 'phos ltas shig gzigs sam ci//'on tam ci zhig de ring 'gro ba shugs kyis 'khrugs gyur tam//'on te 'di na khyed dgra stobs chen 'ga' 
10b8(mdo sde): zhig mchis lags sam//ci yi slad du khyod ni dgyes par mi bzhugs thugs ngan mdzad//bdud kyis smras pa/nga la dgra yod pa ni stobs ldan sems thul ba/sa steng 
11a1(mdo sde): shAkya'i bu pho sgyu ma bslabs pa'i blo//de ni ci nas gal te brlag par ma gyur na//de yis nga yi 'dod khams 'di ni stongs par byed//lha'i bu mo des 
11a2(mdo sde): smras pa//gtso bo srid gsum bcings srid rgya mtsho mtha' rings la//zad bgyid nus pa'i stobs dang ldan pa su zhig mchis//bdag po thabs stobs brtson 'grus rtsal 
11a3(mdo sde): rnams gang gis kyang //de la de ring ma lus bgyid par nus ma mchis//bdud kyis smras pa/sbyin dang brtul zhugs bsam pa smon lam snying rje'i zhags pa can//stong pa 
11a4(mdo sde): mtshan ma med pa'i mtshon cha mchog dang gzhu thogs te//'khor ba las byung rab tu zhi ba'i lam dang 'thun gyur nas//srid pa ma lus bzlog pa nye bar bstan 
11a5(mdo sde): pa byed pa po//de'i slob ma bsam gtan la ni mngon pa ra brtson yid kyis//bden par rgyu zhing nyes pa zad par bya phyir khor zug tu//cho ga bzhin du zhugs nas grong dang 
11a6(mdo sde): grong khyer stong ba dang //nags kyi nang dag dang ni ri'i sman ljongs rnams na gnas/rdzu 'phrul dang ni stobs dang snying rje'i grogs dang ldan pa yi//dam pa nye rgyal pang nas skyes gnyis thub 
11a7(mdo sde): pas btul bar gyur//rab dul chos can 'jig rten gsum kun cho ga bzhin du btul//des ni nga'i 'dod khams 'di dag stong par byed par 'gyur//de nas bdud kyi bu mo 
11a8(mdo sde): lnga brgya po de dag gis bdud sdig can las/bcom ldan 'das kyi yon tan gyi sngags pa thos nas thams cad kyis byang chub sems dpa' ting nge 'dzin rnam pa dang bral ba'i 
11b1(mdo sde): glog ces bya ba thob bo//de nas bdud kyi bu mo lnga brgya po de dag gis/bcom ldan 'das la mchod pa bya ba'i phyir//lha'i sil snyan dang/lha'i me tog dang/bdug pa dang/spos 
11b2(mdo sde): dang /phreng ba dang/byug pa dang/lhab lhub dang/spud pa dang/rgyan de dag bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logasu gtor ba dang/lha'i me tog dang /sil snyan nas rgyan gyi bar du 
11b3(mdo sde): de dag bcom ldan 'das kyi rdzu 'phrul gyi mthus 'ad ma'i tshal du bab par gyur to//bdud kyi bu mo de dag kyang g.yog dang bcas pas so so nas/bcom ldan 'das 
11b4(mdo sde): mthong ngo //de dag gis mthong nas kyang rab tu dga' ba deng rangs pa skyes par gyur to//gang gi phyir 'od ma'i tshal du 'di lta bu'i me tog gi char rab tu bab ces 
11b5(mdo sde): dge slong de dag the tshom skyes nas /bcom ldan 'das la gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das shA ri'i bu dang/mo'u dgal gyi bu g.yog dang bcas pa 'di 
11b6(mdo sde): gnyis la ngo mtshar dang rmadu byung ba chen po sngon ma mthong ma thos pa'i me tog gi char chen po rab tu bab pa lags/bcom ldan 'das 'di'i rgyu ci lags /rkyen gang 
11b7(mdo sde): lags/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu 'di gnyis kyis mthu ma yin te/bdud sdig can gyi g.yog mo lnga brgya tsam gyis //bdud kyi khang pa de nas
11b8(mdo sde): nga la mchod pa bya ba'i phyir 'di lta bu'i me tog gi chur chen po nas rgyan gyi bar gyi char phab bo//de dag ring po mi thogs par 'dir 'ongs te/nga las bla na med pa yang dag 
12a1(mdo sde): par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu lung bstan pa 'thob bo//de nas bdud kyi bu mo lnga brgya po de dag so so nas/bcom ldan 'das kyi gsung dang /brjod 
12a2(mdo sde): pa'i dbyangs thos nas/bcom ldan 'das la de bas kyang rab tu dga' ba skyes te/de dga' ba dang /rangs pa des byang chub kyi sems mi brjed pa zhes bya ba'i tinge 'dzin 
12a3(mdo sde): thob bo//de nas yang bdud kyi bu mo de dag bdud kyi khang pa der gos phrag pa gcig tu bgos te/pus mo g.yas pa'i lha nga sa la btsugs nas thal mo sbyar te/bcom ldan 
12a4(mdo sde): 'das phyogs gang na bzhugs pa'i phyogs der bltas nas 'di skad ces gsol to//sred chu ma lus skams pa 'gro ba'i mig cig pa//mig ma mchis pa'i 'jig rten 
12a5(mdo sde): kun la gzigs nas ni//khyed dang lha dang mir bcas pa yi 'gro ba sgrol//thub pa bdag cag ji ltar myur du sangs rgyas 'gyur//lha dang mis mchod don 
12a6(mdo sde): dam gsung pa bcom ldan 'das//bdag cag 'thun par bud med smad pa nyid spangs nas//blo mchog khyod kyi 'phrul gyis drung du myur bar ni//mchis nas 'di 
12a7(mdo sde): bzhin thub pa'i dbang po'i gsung mnyan to//bdag med gsung ba don dam gzigs pa bcom ldan 'das//byang chub yan lag rin chen dri med nor gsung sgron//
12a8(mdo sde): mtshungs med bdud kyi stobs btul khyod kyis bdag cag la//da ltar myur du lhan cig byang chub lung bstan gsol//de nas bdud kyi bu mo de dag stan las langs te mgrin gcig 
12b1(mdo sde): tu bdud sdig can la 'di skad ces smraso//nyes byas blo gros can khyod snying po med g.yo ba'i//dpal rnyed ci'i phyir bcom ldan 'das la sdang bar sems//dregs pas gos nas 
12b2(mdo sde): skye ba la sogs sdug bsngal gyis//lus kun gtses te mi bzad gnas su nye bar song //de ltar khro ba spongs te rgyal la dad par gyis//'khor ba'i nyes pa dreg pa'i 
12b3(mdo sde): 'dam las thar ba'i bdag/'di ni nyid kyis 'gro ba kun gyi ngo bo mkhyen//thugs rje can gyi skyabs su 'dong gis myur du bzhud//de nas bdud sdig can shin tu 'dug pa'i yid 
12b4(mdo sde): dang ldan pas 'di snyam du sems te/bdag gis 'di 'dra ba'i bdud kyi stobs kyi yul drag po dran par byas la/gang g.yog mo lnga brgya tsam po 'di dag bdag nyid 
12b5(mdo sde): bcing ba lngas bcings par mthong bar byas te/'di nas bzlog la 'gro mi nus par bya'o snyam pa dang /bdud des de dag bcing ma nus so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar 
12b6(mdo sde): g.yog mo lnga brgya tsam po de dag de bzhin gshegs pas byin gyis brlabs pa'i phyir ro//de nas g.yog mo lnga brgya tsam de dag bdud sdig can gyi drung nas dong ba dang /de nas 
12b7(mdo sde): bdud sdig can rab tu khros te 'di snyam du sems so//bdag gis yang 'di 'dra ba'i bdud kyi stongs kyi yul drag po dran pa byas te/ci nas bdag cag gis g.yog mo 
12b8(mdo sde): de dag phyogs dang phyogs mtshams thams cad 'khrul nas dge sbyong go'u tam mi mthong zhing slar yang bdag gis gnas su 'ong bar nam mkha' 'di dag thams cad du rnam par 
13a1(mdo sde): mthor rlung dang /rlung nag po chen pos kun khyab par bya'o snyam na'ang /sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs stobs kyis rlung cung zad kyang langs par 
13a2(mdo sde): byed ma nus so//tha na skra'i rtse mo gcig tsam yang bskyod ma nus na mang du lta ci smos/de nas yang bdud sdig can rab tu khros shing sdug bsngal te yid mi bde zhing yid 
13a3(mdo sde): la bcags nas/du shing skad chen pos rang gi bu dang /tshogs kyi 'khor la bos te/bdud kyi gnas thams cad sgras gang bar byas so//sdug pa'i bu dang tshogs 
13a4(mdo sde): kyi 'khor rnams tshur sheg shig/nga ni bdag yul rang gi rdzu 'phrul stobs las nyams//shAkya'i bu 'di/sgyu ldan g.yon can ngag 'jam la//khong nas rang bzhin 
13a5(mdo sde): dug sngor 'dra ba 'dir skyes so//de nas sgra des bdud kyi g.yog mo dang /bdud kyi bu mo de dag thams cad dang /bdud kyi bu tshogs kyi 'khor dang bcas pa de dag thams cad 
13a6(mdo sde): rings pa'i gzugs kyis myur du lhags nas/bdud sdig can gyi mdun du 'khod pa dang /'khor de'i nang nas bdud kyi bu rgyal ba'i blo gros shes bya ba des thal mo sbyar te 'di skad ces 
13a7(mdo sde): smras so//ci'i slad thugs khros rab tu 'khrugs shing thugs mi dgyes//sreg bskal deng 'dir ma byung 'di nas khyod ma 'phos//'di na khyod lhags dgra ni su yang ma 
13a8(mdo sde): mchis na/khyod ni rmongs par gyur tam ci'i slad gzhan du dgongs//bdud kyis smras pa/shAkya'i bu pho dmangs phal shing gi grib ma 'dug pa ma mthong ngam/ci'i phyir 'di 
13b1(mdo sde): na khyod kyi mdun na stobs dang ldan pa'i dgra med smras//stobs gcig ldan pa g.yon can de yis thams cad rnam pa du mar bslus//bdag cag bu dang sder bcas me lce 'bar 
13b2(mdo sde): dang bcas pa'i me yis bsregs//'jig rten 'di na skyes bu gtso bo dpal dang brjod pa rnam grags pa//mkhas pa bstan bcos snyan dngags mang po byed brtson 'thun pa myur 
13b3(mdo sde): bar yang //chos kyi kyo ba btang gis dwangs te shAkya'i bu 'di'i skyabs der song //lus mdzes g.yo can blo gros ldan 'di nga'i dgrar ni rab tu gnang //rab tu sdug pa'i g.yog 
13b4(mdo sde): mo de dag snying rje med pas nga bor te//nga la dbang gtsugs nas ni de ring dge slong de'i skyabs su dong //gal te ba dag cag myur bsgrims stobs chen 'di deng thal bar ma 
13b5(mdo sde): byas na//g.yo dang rgyu yis srid pa gsum po de dag ma lus stongs par byed//de nas bdud kyi bu tsho gas kyi 'khor dang bcas pa de dag thams cad kyis thal 
13b6(mdo sde): mo sbyar te 'di skad ces smras so//de ltar bgyi'o//bdag cag gi rdzu 'phrul dang /stobs kyi yul gyi mthu dang /rnam par sprul pa de dag thams cad bstan te/shAkya'i 
13b7(mdo sde): bu 'di gal te thal bar bgyid nus pa lta na ni legs de ltar ma nus na ni de'i skyabs su mchi'o//yab nyid kyi mngon sum du gyur pa lags te//gang bdag cag sde chen pos 
13b8(mdo sde): yongs su bskor ba/sngar shAkya'i bu gcig pu gnyis su med pa 'di rdzu 'phrul gyi stobs kyis rab tu pham par bgyis na/da g.yog mang por gyur pa lta ci smos//bdud sdig 
14a1(mdo sde): can gyis smras pa/bzhin bzang dag deng la/gal te dge sbyong go'u ta ma 'di gsod nus na slar sheg shig/ji ste ma nus na yang bdag cag gi 
14a2(mdo sde): gnas kyang bsrung gis slar sheg shig/bdud kyi 'khor dkrigs phrag bcu gnyis po de dag de nas 'phags te//'di nas steng du dpag tshad brgyad khri bzhi stong gi bar du 
14a3(mdo sde): bkang nas//de 'dra ba'i bdud kyi stobs kyis yul gyi rdzu 'phrul drag po bstan te/gang gling bzhi pa'i nam mkha' thams cad sprin nag po chen po dang /rlung nag po chen po dang 
14a4(mdo sde): skar mdas bkang nas//ri'i rgyal po ri rab la yang la/ga gis brdabs te//gling bzhi pa thams cad/rab tu g.yo bar byas nas/shin tu 'jigs pa'i sgra 
14a5(mdo sde): rnams kyang 'byin to//de dag gis klu dang /klu chen po dang /gnod sbyin dang /gnod sbyin chen po dag gis sa chen po brag dang rir bcas pa thams cad dang ldan pa/ri'i 
14a6(mdo sde): rgyal po ri rab kyang g.yos par rig/mtsho dang mtsho chen po dang /'bab chu dang /chu bran dang /'bab chu chen po dang /rgya mtsho chen po rnams kyang 'khrugs par rig nas/
14a7(mdo sde): nam mkha'i dkyil na 'khod do//bdud kyi 'khor de dag ni ri rab kyi zom la 'khod nas/dpag tshad tsam gyi rdo'i char pa rnam par sprul te//nam mkha' las kun tu 'bebs so//
14a8(mdo sde): ral gri dang /gtun shing dang /rdo ba dang /mtshon rtse gnyis pa dang /ste'u kha ma dang /lcags mda' dang /spu gri dang /spu gri lta bu dang /dgra sta dang ste'u so dang /kha rad 
14b1(mdo sde): rod can dang /kha shin tu rad rod can /sra ba/drag pa/rtsub pa rno ba'i char rab tu sprul te kun tu phab bo//de nas de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das bdud kyi 'khor rnam par 'jig pa zhes bya 
14b2(mdo sde): ba'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs te /gang rdo dang mtshon cha de dag thams cad lha'i me tog ut+pa la dang /pad+ma dang /ku mu dang /pad+ma dkar po dang /man da ra ba dang /man 
14b3(mdo sde): dar ba chen po'i me tog gi char chen por byin gyis brlabs/yul ang ga ma d+har skar mnga' ltung ba ni spos kyi char chen por byin gyis rlabs /sgra de dag ni rol mo'i sgra 
14b4(mdo sde): sna tshogs byin gyis brlabs te//'di ltar sangs rgyas kyi sgra dang /chos kyi sgra dang /dge 'dun gyi sgra dang /pha rol tu phyin pa'i sgra dang /mngon par shes pa'i sgra dang /
14b5(mdo sde): phyir mi ldog pa'i sgra dang /dbang bskur ba'i sgra dang /bdud bzhi pham par bya ba'i sgra dang /byang chub kyi snying por 'gro ba'i sgra dang /len pa dang bcas pa dang /
14b6(mdo sde): len pa med pa'i bar gyi sgrar byin gyis brlabs so//rdul dang mun ga dang /rlung thad kyang rab tu zhi'o//gling bzhi pa 'di na rtsa dang /shing gel pa dang /sman dang /nags tshal dang /sa dang 
14b7(mdo sde): brag dang /ri ci yod pa de dag thams cad rin po che sna bdun gyis brlabs so//bcom ldan 'das gtsug tor bltar mi mthong ba dang ldan pa ni tshangs pa'i 'jig rten gyi bar 
14b8(mdo sde): du skus dbang mdzad do//bcom ldan 'das kyi mtshan re re las kyang 'di 'dra 'od byung ste//'od nges stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams snang ba chen pos 
15a1(mdo sde): @#//khyab par gyur to//stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di'i lha dang /klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' 
15a2(mdo sde): lding dang /mi 'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po dang /yi dwags dang sha za dang /grul bum dang /mi dang mi ma yin pa dang /sems can dmyal ba dang /dud 'gro'i skyes gnas 
15a3(mdo sde): dang /gshin rje'i 'jig rten pa de dag rnams kyi bcom ldan 'das mthong nas lha dang /klu dang gnod sbyin dang /dri za dag/lha ma yin dang /nam mkha'i lding dang /mi 'am 
15a4(mdo sde): ci dang /lto 'phye chen pa dang /mi dang mi ma yinapa sa dang /nam mkha' la 'khod pa brgya stong mang pos ni me tog/gtor cing bstod nas bskor ba byas so//sems 
15a5(mdo sde): can dmyal ba pa dang /dud 'gro'i skye gnas pa dang /gshin rje'i 'jig rte pa mi 'khrugs pa phrag bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong mang po ni dran pa rnyed de/dge ba'i rtsa ba sngon bskyed 
15a6(mdo sde): pa dran nas/sangs rgyas la phyag 'tshal lo zhes byas te/ngan song de dag nas shi 'phos nas lhar skyes so//bdud kyi sde de'i nang nas/bdud kyi bu nye khri tshogs kyi 
15a7(mdo sde): 'khor dang bcas pas/bcom ldan 'das kyi cho 'phrul chen po 'di lta bu mthong nas bcom ldan 'das la rab tu dga' bar gyur te/bcom ldan 'das ga la der dong nas phyin 
15a8(mdo sde): pa dang /bdud kyi bu mo lnga brgya dang lhan cig tu bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te thal mo sbyar nas tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rnam dag sku mnga' 
15b1(mdo sde): mchog tu gzugs bzang pa/ye shes rgya mtsho gser gyi lhun po 'dra//grags pa 'jig rten gsum na lam me ba//mgon po khyod nyid la ni skyabasu mchi//rab tu 
15b2(mdo sde): lam stor rnam par mi gzums la//khyod ni 'jig rten sgron ma'i nyi ma 'dra//gzhan gyis mi thub srog chags rtsa lag gcig/ded dpon khyod la skyabs su rab tu 
15b3(mdo sde): mchi ༐ shin tu tshogs bsags ye shes 'byor pa'i mdzod//namakha'i rang bzhin gzod nas rnam grol thugs//thugs rje'i bsam pa yid 'ong 'jam pa'i gsung//
15b4(mdo sde): don kun grub pa khyod la skyabs su mchi//'khor ba dgon pa las kyang khyod thar mdzad//tshogs pa las kyang rgyu dang 'bras bu ston//byams la gnas shing mchog 
15b5(mdo sde): gi cho ga mkhyen//thugs rje'i gnas pa'i skyabs su rab tu mchi//sgyu ma smig rgyu chu zla 'dra ba yis//srid pa la ni yul gyi gnas kyis chags//mi 
15b6(mdo sde): shes nad sel 'jig rten rnams kyi mgon//sman pa'i rgyal po khyod la skyabs su mchi//chu bo bzhi dbusa zam par gyur pa khyod//nor bdun 'phags pa'i 
15b7(mdo sde): tshul gyis rab sgrol ba//yang dag lam ston 'jig rten rtsa lag po//thugs rje ldan pa khyod la 'dir mchod do//blo mchog khyod la bdag cag bzod ba gsol 
15b8(mdo sde): khyod la bdag cag yid kyis gnod bsams pa//mgon po dpa' bos nongs pa bzod par gsol//khyod ni 'gro ba'i rtsa lag gcig pu gtso//bdag cag 
16a1(mdo sde): bdud kyi phyogs rnams rab spangs te//byang chub mchog gi sems ni bskyed par bgyi//sems can ma lus thams cad mgron du gnyer//
16a2(mdo sde): bdag cag gis ni byang chub mchog thob bgyi//ci nas bdag cag pha rol phyin spyod par//bdag cag rnams la spyod pa rgya chen ston//gzhan ma yin gsungs chos 
16a3(mdo sde): ni du rnams dang //ldan na byang chub rab tu 'thob par 'gyur//me tog gang gis khyod la mngon gtor ba//phyogs kun tu yang de dag gdugs gyur te//dus kun bde 
16a4(mdo sde): ba'i 'byung gnas zhing du ni//rkang gnyis mchog gi spyi bor 'dug gyur cig/de nas bdud kyi bu mo de dag dang /bdud kyi bu tshogs kyi 'khor dang bcas pa de dag thams cad 
16a5(mdo sde): kyis/bcom ldan 'das la me tog sil ma gtor ba dang //me tog sil ma de dag/bcom ldan 'das kyi rdzu 'phrul gyi mthus gang ga'i klung bye ba khrag 
16a6(mdo sde): khrig brgya stong gi bye ma las 'das pa'i me tog gi gdugs dkar po kun tu 'dug par gyur te/me tog gi gdugs de dag kyang /phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
16a7(mdo sde): 'das bzhugs shing 'tsho la gzhes pa thams cad kyi dbu'i gtsug gi drang thad kyi steng gi bar snang la 'dug go/bdud kyi bu mo de dag dang/bdud kyi bu tshogs kyi tshor 
16a8(mdo sde): dang bcas pa de dag gis kyang so so nas phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing grangs med dpag tu med pa thams cad na sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das bzhugs shing 'tsho la/
16b1(mdo sde): gzhes te/chos kyang 'chad pa 'khor gyis yongs su bskor te lham me/lhan ne/lhang nger bzhugs pa'i dbu'i gtsug gi drang thad kyi steng gi bar snang la me 
16b2(mdo sde): tog gi gdugs de dag 'dagu par gyur pa mthong zhing sangs rgyas bcom ldan de dag kyang kha dog mtshungs pa/rtags mtshungs pa/gzugs mtshungs par snang ba sha stag la/sangs rgyas bcom 
16b3(mdo sde): ldan 'das de dag seng ge'i khri tha dad cing 'khor tha dad la sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi yon tan bkod pa nye tshe tha dad par mthong ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gi dbyangs kyi dkyil 
16b4(mdo sde): 'khor gyi gsung brjod pa ni ma thos//bdud kyi 'khor de dag bcom ldan 'das kyis mthus 'di lta bu'i cho 'phrul mthong nas/mchog tu dga' ba dang/mos 
16b5(mdo sde): pa skyes te/bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal nas chos mnyan pa'i phyir spyan sngar 'khod do//de nas bdud kyi bu tshogs kyi 'khor dang 
16b6(mdo sde): bcas pa de dag las dkrigs phrags bcu phyir logs nas/bdud kyi gnas su de lta bu'i ngo mtshar bdud sdig can la rgya cher bzlas te dge sbyong go'u ta ma de'i spu'i khung 
16b7(mdo sde): bu gcig kyang bdag cag gis gzhig ma nus na/gsad par lta ci smos/de'i steng du nyi khri de'i skyabs su dong ste/chos mnyan pa'i phyir de'i mdun na 'khod do//de 
16b8(mdo sde): nas bdud sdig can rab tu khros te/sdug bsngal zhing yid mi bde nas/yid la bcags te/des 'di skad ces smras so/shAkya'i bu 'di ji srid brlag par ma byas pa/
17a1(mdo sde): @#//de srid bar du nga yi dpal song slar mi 'ong//shAkya'i bu 'di ji ltar de ring gsad snyam ste//bdag cag gzhan la yid mi rtog cing mi smra 'dug//
17a2(mdo sde): de nas bdud sdig can yid mi bde zhing mya ngan gyi khang pa na 'dug go////'dus pa chen po rin po che tog gi gzungs las/bdud spa bkong ba'i le'u
17a3(mdo sde): ste dang po'o////de nas bdud kyi bu mo dang/bdud kyi bu pho tshogs kyi 'khor dang bcas pa de dag gis/bcom ldan 'das la 'di ces gsol
17a4(mdo sde): to|bcom ldan 'das bdag cag ni 'di lta bu'i thega pa dang/ye shes dang/rdzu 'phrul dang/snying rje dang/thabs dang/spobs pa 'tshal lo//bcom
17a5(mdo sde): ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thabs dang ye sheskyi bar du ldan pa ngo mtshar to//bcom ldan 'das chos du dang ldan na/skyes bu gang zag 
17a6(mdo sde): sdig pa'i drogs po'i lag tu mi mchi zhing myur du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu ni mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya/bcom ldan 'das kyis 
17a7(mdo sde): bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu dag chos bzhi dang ldan na/'di la skyes bu gang zag la la sdig pa'i grogs po'i lag tu mi 'gro zhing//myur du bla na med 
17a8(mdo sde): pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'ibyang chub tu mngon bar rdzogs par 'tshang rgya'o//bzhi gang zhe na/bzhin bzang dag 'di la rigs kyi bu chos thams cad la 
17b1(mdo sde): mchog tu mi 'dzin cing //chos gang yang mi 'dzin/mi gtong /khas mi len/mi gnas/nye bar mi len/mi rtog rnam par mi rtog go/'di lta ste/sbyin 
17b2(mdo sde): pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa na/sbyin pa'i 'bras bu yang mi gtong mi 'dzin/khas mi len/nye bar mi len/miganas/mi rtog ste/shes rab kyi 
17b3(mdo sde): rol tu phyin pa'i bar du spyod pa na/mi rtog rnam par mi ldog pa'i bar du'o//bzhin bzangs dag gzhan yang rigs kyi bu de sems can du smra ba ma yin/
17b4(mdo sde): srog tu smra ba ma yin//gang zag tu smra ba ma yin/sems can gyi khams la yid kyis mchog tu mi 'dzin pa nas/mi rtog rnam par mi rtag pa'i 
17b5(mdo sde): bar du'o//bzhin bzang dag gzhan yang rigsa kyi bu de gzugs dang/sgra dang/dri dang/ro dang/reg bya la mchog tu mi 'dzin pa na/mi 
17b6(mdo sde): rtog rnam par mi rtog pa'i bar du'o//bzhin bzang dgazhan yang rigs kyi bu de khams gsum dang/dus gsum dang/phung po dang/khams dang/skye mched 
17b7(mdo sde): rnams kyi rgyu dang rkyen dang/dmigs pa dang/'bras bu rnam par smin pa 'byung ba dang/gnas dang/skye ba thams cad la mchog tu mi 'dzin pa nas/mi rtog 
17b8(mdo sde): rnam par mi rtog pa'i bar dngu'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes ni spyod pa'i gnas kyis lus thams cad la rtog pa dang/rnam par rtog 
18a1(mdo sde): pa dang bral ba ste/mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis spyad par bya'o//bzhin bzangs dag chos thams cad dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid ni 
18a2(mdo sde): dngos po med pa yin te/sgra med pa/mtshan ma med pa/yi ge med pa/smon pa med/skye ba med pa/'gag pa med pa/mtshan nyid med pa/chags pa med pa/
18a3(mdo sde): dmigs pa med pa/bstan du med pa/dben pa/bdag med pa/mtshan nyid dang bral ba/skad cig pa/nye bar zhi ba/mun pa med pa/snang ba med pa/gnas 
18a4(mdo sde): med pa/yul med pa/dbang po med pa/phyogs med pa/gnyen po med pa/bsam du med pa/mi 'phogs pa/ser sna med pa/spos pa med pa/
18a5(mdo sde): rdul med pa/rdul dang bral ba/cha shas med pa/ci'ang med pa/byed pa po med pa/tshor ba po med pa/rten na med pa/gzung ba med pa/rnam par rig pa med 
18a6(mdo sde): pa/so sor snang ba med pa/skad cig pa ma yin pa'i bar du'o//bzhin bzangs dag thams cad mkhyen pa nyid nam mkha' dang mtshungs te/stong pa nyid pas dmigs 
18a7(mdo sde): su med pa dang /mi gnas pa'i tshul dang /mchog tu 'dzin pa'i tshul dang /mi rtog rnam par mi rtog pa'i tshul gyis spyad par bya'o//bzhin bzangs dag chos bzhi 
18a8(mdo sde): po de dag dang ldan na/skyes bu gang zag sdig pa'i grog po'i lag tu ma song zhing /myur du bla na med pa yang rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya'o//
18b1(mdo sde): bzhin bzang dag gang la la yul thams cad la chags pa'i lus kyi mtshan nyid kyi gnas la rnam par spyod pas/thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes tshol na/de gnyis la 
18b2(mdo sde): chags pa yin te/de gnyis la chags pa'i yid kyi brnag pa ni/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid slu bar byed do//de la gnyis gang zhe na/gang phung po dang /
18b3(mdo sde): khams dang /skye mched rnams la mtshan nyid du rnam par dpyod pas gnas pa dang /mchog tu 'dzin pa de ni gnyis te/thams cad mkhyen pa nying slu ba'o//spyod pa'i gnas 
18b4(mdo sde): dang /'bras bu la rtog pa'i 'di ni gnyiso/skye ba dang srid pa dang /len pa dang /sems can gyi gnas la rtog pa 'di ni gnyiso//bshad pa dang rab tu bstan pa dang gdams 
18b5(mdo sde): pa dang /tshig gi lam dang /sgra dang /skad kyi gnas la rtog pa 'di ni nyis so//rtag pa dang /chad pa la rnam par lta ba'i shes pa dang /shes bya'i 
18b6(mdo sde): gnas la rtog pa 'di ni gnyas so//sems can dang /srog dang /gso ba dang /skyes bu dang /gang zag dang /byed pa po dang /byed du 'jug pa po'i 'du shes 
18b7(mdo sde): kyi gnas la rtog pa 'di na gnyis so//gang pha rol dang /tsho rol dang /rtog pa dang /sel ba la gnas shing rtog pa de ni gnyis so//bzhin bzangs dag skyes bu gang 
18b8(mdo sde): zag su la thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes tshol ba de dus gsum du ngar 'dzin pa dang /nga yir 'dzin pa dang /kun 'byung ba dang /'gog par dpyod pa la gnas shing 
19a1(mdo sde): kun tu rtog ste/mchog tu 'dzin na 'di ni thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes la de 'di gnyis so//'di lta ste dper na skyes bu la la zhig me 
19a2(mdo sde): 'dod pa la sa'i ngos la nyug pa dang /skom 'dod pa la me dang /zas 'dod pa la rdo dang /me tog 'dod pa la gos dang /spos 'dod pa la mi ro bam pa dang /gos 'dod 
19a3(mdo sde): pa la dur khrod dang /byug pa 'dod pa la nam mkha' la nyug pa de bzhin du/bzhin bzangs dag gang spyod pa'i gnas la chags shing rnar spyod pa'i lus gnyis kyi gnas kyi thams 
19a4(mdo sde): cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes tshol na/de'i 'bad pa 'bras bu med pa yin no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po gzungs kyi blo gros shes bya ba 
19a5(mdo sde): 'khor der 'dus par gyur nas 'dug pa des/bcom ldan 'das ga la ba de logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud de 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 
19a6(mdo sde): 'das gang brjod du ma mchis pa de mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar nus lags sam/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/gang brjod du med par shes pa de nyid 
19a7(mdo sde): mngon par rdzogs par 'tshangs rgya ba'o//de'i phyir rigs kyi bu khyod nyid la 'dris/khyod kyis ji ltar bzod pa de bzhin du lan thob shig/gang thams cad mkhyen pa'i ming 
19a8(mdo sde): thob par 'gyur ba'i rdzas kyi mtshan nyid kyi dngos po gang yang yod dam/gsol pa/gal te mchis shes bgyis na ni rtag par 'gyur//ci ste ma mchis shes bgyis 
19b1(mdo sde): na ni chad par 'gyuro//dbu ma'i lam ni dmigs su ma mchis te/de ni mchis pa yang ma lags ma mchis pa 'ang ma lags so//gang ma chags mi skye mi 'gog/
19b2(mdo sde): tshad ma mchis/grangs ma mchis/dpe ma mchis/snang ba ma mchis pa de la 'tshal ba 'di ni mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya'o//byang chub sems dpa' klog
19b3(mdo sde): gi blo gros kyis gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das gang la 'ang 'gro ba 'ang ma mchis/'ong ba 'ang ma mchis pa de de lta bu shes pa la 'jug par mkhas pa 'di ni
19b4(mdo sde): mngon par rdzogs par 'tshad rgya'o//byang chub sems pa dpa' rnam par snang byed kyis gsol pa/bcom ldana'adas gang na thob pa'i mtshan nyid kyang ma mchis ma
19b5(mdo sde): thob pa'i mtshan nyid kyang mchis/mngon par rtogs pa yang ma mchis/mngon du bgyi ba yang ma mchis/zhi ba yang ma mchis/rab tu zhi ba yang
19b6(mdo sde): ma mchis/dus gsum yang ma mchis/theg pa gsum yang ma mchis/smon lam dang mnyam pa'i rlom sems kyang ma mchis pa 'di ni/mngon par rdzogs
19b7(mdo sde): par 'tshang rgya ba'o//byang chub sems dpa'i gzungs kyi blo gros kyis gsol pa//bcom ldan 'das gang khams gsum yang ma mchis/kun tu sbyor ba
19b8(mdo sde): gsum yang ma mchis/rig pa gsum yang ma mchis /theg pa gsum yang ma mtshis/phung po dang khams dang /skye mched la rtog pa yang ma mchis/
20a1(mdo sde): rnam par rtog pa yang ma mchis/bri ba yang ma mchis/phel ba yang ma mchis la/mnyam par yang mi bgyid pa 'di ni mngon par rdzogs/
20a2(mdo sde): par 'tshang rgya ba'o//byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje blo gros kyis gsol pa/gang so so'i skye bo'i chos dang/'phags pa'i chos dang/slob pa'i chos dang/mi 
20a3(mdo sde): slob pa'i chos dang/nyan thos kyi chos dang/rang sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams la mi rtogs par mi rtog ste/mnyam par yang mi bgying pa 'di ni mngon par rjogs par 
20a4(mdo sde): 'tshang rgya ba'o//byang chub kyi sems dpa' sra ba'i/blo gros kyis gsol pa/gangabaden pa'i tshul gyis de bzhin nyid la rnam par dpyod cing mngon par mi 
20a5(mdo sde): zhen pa 'di ni mngon par rdzog par 'tshang rgya ba'o//byangachub sems dpa' lag na rin po ches gsol pa/gang chos thams cad kyi mi skye ba'i mtshan nyid 
20a6(mdo sde): thob par bgyi ba dang/mngon par rtogs par bgyi ba'i slad du mi rtog pa 'di ni mngon par rdzos par 'tshang rgya ba'o//byang cub sems dpa' blo gros bsam gyis mi 
20a7(mdo sde): khyab pas gsol pa/gang khams gsum la rnam par dpyod cing sems nyid sems la 'jug par bgyid de/sems gnyi ga la sems kyi rnam par dpyod nas 
20a8(mdo sde): mi dmigs pa 'di ni mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya ba'o//byang chub sems dpa' dgra las rnam par rgyal gyis gsol pa/gang chos tham cad la mi chags//
20b1(mdo sde): rjes su mi chags btang snyoms su mi bgyid/khong khro bar mi bgyid/'dod par mi bgyid/mi rmongs mi 'dzin/mi gtong ba 'di ni mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang 
20b2(mdo sde): rgya ba'o//byang chub sems dpa' pad+ma'i snying pos gsol pa/gang bsod nams dang sdig pa la mi chags shing zab mo la bzod pa'i tshul la 'jug pas/ngar 'dzin 
20b3(mdo sde): pa dang /nga yir 'dzin pa la mi rtog pa 'di ni mngon par rdzogs bar 'tshang rgya ba'o//zla 'od gzhon nur gyur pas gsol pa/gang chos thams cad chu zla dang 
20b4(mdo sde): mtshung par rab tu 'tshal cing /chos rnams kyi ngo bo nyid stsogs pa dang /dbri bar yang mi mthong ba 'di ni mngon par rtogs par 'tshang brgya ba'o//byang chub 
20b5(mdo sde): sems dpa' nam mkha' blo gros gzhon nur gyur pas/gsol pa/gang gi sems dang sems las/byung ba la mun pa dang /snang ba dang /skye ba dang /'jig 
20b6(mdo sde): pa dang /'phel ba dang /'bri ba thams cad mi 'byung ba 'di ni mngon par rjogs par 'tshang rgya ba'o//byang chub sems pa'i byams pas gsol pa/gang gnas 
20b7(mdo sde): pa la gnas pa na/khams gsum pa la gnas pa'i tshangs pa la mi dmigs/mnyam par mi bgyid pa 'di ni mngon par rdzog par 'tshang rgya ba'o//byang
20b8(mdo sde): chub sems dpa' blo gros mi zad pas gsol pa/gang 'khor gsum yongs su dag pas pha rol tu phyin pa rnams la mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis rab tu goms 
21a1(mdo sde): par bgyid cing mi chags mi skyo ba 'di ni mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya ba'o//'jam dpal gzhon nur gyur pas gsol ba/bcom 
21a2(mdo sde): ldan 'das gang chos thams cad la mi chags mi skyo zhing chos zab mo'i tshul yang rab tu 'tshal te/gang rab tu 'tshal pa yang mi len mi mdor sdud par mi bgyid 
21a3(mdo sde): bral bar mi bgyid cing chos gang la yang stsogs pa'am/'bri ba'am/rig pa dang /rnam par grol ba'am/'jig pa'am/'bri ba'am/'phel ba 
21a4(mdo sde): yang mi skyed la dngos po dag la rnam par mi rtog pas/kun nas nyon mongs par mi 'gyur ba 'di ni mngon par rdzog par 'tshang rgya ba ste/tshul 
21a5(mdo sde): gcig po 'dis thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes mngon par rdzogs par 'chang rgya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' ltad mo can gyis smras pa/'jam 
21a6(mdo sde): dpal de bzhin nyid la 'jug pa'i tshul 'di ltar zab mo stong pa nyid bsgoms pa'i tshul 'di gcig gis thams cad mkhyen pa'i yi shes mngon par rdzogs pa 
21a7(mdo sde): 'tshang rgya na/kun tu sbyor ba dang /rab tu sbyor ba cir dgos 'jam dpal gyis smras pa/lta ba mi zad pa dang bral zhing yang dag pa'i lta bar sgro 'dogs pa 
21a8(mdo sde): yang med pa dang /g.yo med pa dang /drang por sgro 'dogs pa yang med pa dang /sdig pa dang bral zhing bla ma la ri mor bya bar sgro 'dogs pa yang med ba/bka' 
21b1(mdo sde): blo bde bar sgro 'dogs pa yang med pa dang /yang dag pa'i 'tsho bar sgro 'dogs pa yang mad pa dang /kun tu sbyor ba thams cad dang bral bar sgro 'dogs pa yang me 
21b2(mdo sde): pa dang /sems can thams cad la khro ba dang /snying rje bar sgro 'dogs pa yang myed pa dang /sdom pa gsum du sgro 'dogs pa yang med dang /mi slu ba dge 
21b3(mdo sde): ba'i chos su sgro 'dogs pa yang med de/rnam par ma zhi bar sgro 'dogs pa yang mod pa dang /dam pa'i chos srung bar sgro 'dogs pa yang mid pa dang /sems can 
21b4(mdo sde): thams cad yongs su mi gtong bar sgro 'dogs pa yang yod pa dang /dngos po thams cad yongs su gtong bar sgro 'dogs pa'ang med pa dang /sems can nyams 
21b5(mdo sde): chung ba stobs la rab tu dgod par sgro 'dogs pa yang de med pa dang /'jigs pa'i skyabs mi 'jigs par sgro 'dogs pa yang med/lam 
21b6(mdo sde): ngan par zhugs pa rnams nan tan la gzung bar sgro 'dogs pa yang med pa dang /bzod pa dang /shes par sgro 'dogs pa yang mad pa dang /'dzin pa thams cad 
21b7(mdo sde): la chags pa'i mtshan nyid du sgro 'dogs pa yang medapa dang /rdul dang mun pa'i phung po thams cad spong bar sgro 'dogs pa yang myed pa dang /bsngo ba'i 'bras bu rnam 
21b8(mdo sde): par smin pa thams cad spong bar sgro dogs pa yang med 'di dag ni rigs kyi bu thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi sbyor ba nyi shu ste/yi ge dang /skad dang /sgra dang /
22a1(mdo sde): @#//smra ba dang/brjod pa'i tshig rab tu dbye ba thams cad kyi de bzhin nyid shes pa la 'jug pas thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi sbyor ba'o//
22a2(mdo sde): gzhan mu stegs can gyi tshigs gi de bzhin nyid la 'jug pa dang/spyod pa thams cad kyi de bzhin nyi la 'jug pa dang/bsod nams thams cad dang/shes rab kyi 
22a3(mdo sde): pha rol tu phyin pa la sbyor ba'i de bzhin nyid la 'jug pas/len pa dang/skye ba dang/'jig ba thams cad kyi de bzhinanyid la 'jug pa dang/rnam par thar pa gsum
22a4(mdo sde): dang/gnas dang/rgyu dang las dang/chos thams cad kyi de bzhin yid la 'jug pas/de bzhin gshegs pa'i bka' thams cad mkhyen pa'i yeshes kyi sbyor ba 
22a5(mdo sde): khong du chud par 'gyur ro//byang chub sems dpa' ltad mo can gyis smras pa/'jam dpal de de bzhin te/nam chos zab mo'i tshul 'di rtogs 
22a6(mdo sde): pa de'i tshe/chos gang spong ba dang/gang la spong ba dang/don dang tshigs dang/yi ge gang dag gis ston pa dang/rab tu ston pa'i chos gang yang mi 
22a7(mdo sde): mthong ste/gang rab tu ston pa dang/gang bsgom pa dang/gang kun shes par 'gyur ba'i chos de thams cad brjod du med pa'i tshul gyis de bzhin nyid la 'jug
22a8(mdo sde): ste/des thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes khong du chud par 'gyur ro//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/legs so legs so//rigs kyi bu 
22b1(mdo sde): tshul gcig gis thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes thob pa de khyod kyis legs par smras so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/thams cad la chags pa med pa'i dngos po yin 
22b2(mdo sde): pas/chos thams cad ni sgro btags pa med pa dang /ma skyes pa dang /mi 'jigs pa'i mtha' can dang /ma rigs pa dang /mya ngan las 'das pa'i mi 
22b3(mdo sde): skye ba'i yang dag pa'i mtha' can dang /nam mkha' dang mya ngan las 'das pa'i mtha' can dang /chos thams cad ni brjod du med pa'i mtha' can dang /sems can 
22b4(mdo sde): thams cad kyang de lta bu yin pa dang /chos thams cad ni rdzas med pa'i mtha' can/dus gsum dang /khams gsum dang /phung po thams cad ni ci 
22b5(mdo sde): yang med pa'i mtha' can dang/'du byed gsum ni stong pa nyid kyi mtha' can dang /chos kyi phung po dang /rnam par smin pa'i phung po dang /stsog 
22b6(mdo sde): pa dang /bri ba'i phung po ni rdzas med pa'i mtha' cad du yongs su brjod do//stong pa nyid dang /yang dag pa'i mtha' dang /brjod du med pa'i don dang /
22b7(mdo sde): chos thams cad kyi don gyi chos thams cad dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po ni thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes la 'jug go/thams 
22b8(mdo sde): cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes 'thob pa lung bstan pa 'di bshad pa'i tshe/bdud kyi bu dang /bdud kyi bu mo tshogs kyi 'khor nyi khris mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa thob 
23a1(mdo sde): @#//ste lus rags pa spangs nas yid kyi lus rnyed do//gzhan yang nyi khri brgyad stong gis mi skye ba'i chos la bzod pa thob par gyur to//lha dang 
23a2(mdo sde): dkrigs phrag dgu bcu rtsa gnyis kyi ni byang chub sems dpa'i bzod pa dang/ting nge 'dzin dang/gzungs sna tshogs thob par gyur to//de nas byang chub sems dpa'
23a3(mdo sde): sems dpa' chen po mi skye ba'i chos la bzong pa thob pa nyi khri bo de dag gis/bcom ldan 'das la lha'i me tog gi gtor/rab tu gtor nas bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs 
23a4(mdo sde): la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te/'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das 'di ltar mi dge ba'i grogs po dang 'grogs pa'i dbang gis/sems can rnams
23a5(mdo sde): bsod nams thams cad stsogs pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba yida la mi bgyid pa la gzigs su gsol //bcoma ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/'di ni las 
23a6(mdo sde): kyi rkyen yin par blta'o//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis ngo mtshar du gyur sa'i sems can rnams kyi the tsom gcad pa'i phyir sngon byung ba 'di gsungs so//rigs 
23a7(mdo sde): kyi bu sngon byung ba 'das pa'i dus na bskal pa chen po dpag tu med pa 'das pa de'i tshe de'i dus na/gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams 'di nyid du snang ba 'char ba zhes bya 
23a8(mdo sde): ba'i bskal pa chen po la/skye dgu'i tshe lo drug khri brgyad stong thub ste/de'i tshe de'i dus na/de bzhin gshegs pa 'od zhi spos snang dpal zhes bya ba rigs pa 
23b1(mdo sde): dang zhabs su ldan pa/bde bar gshegs pa 'jig rten mkhyen pa/skyes bu 'dul ba'i kha lo sgyur ba/bla na med pa lha dang mi rnams kyi ston pa sangs rgyas bcom ldan

23b3(mdo sde): ba'i 'khor lo sgyur ba'i rgyal po ud pa la'i gdong zhes bya ba zhig byung /de nas dus gzhan zhig gis tshe/rgyal po ud pa la'i gdod btsun mo'i 'khor dang /g.yog 
23b4(mdo sde): dang /dpung gi tshogs dang bcas te/de bzhin gshegs pa 'od zhi spos snang dpal ga la ba der song nas/phyin pa dang /bcom ldan 'das kyi zhabs la mgo

23b7(mdo sde): 'di dag gis mngon par stod do//lha dang klu la sogs pa'i yon tan gyis//mngon par rab bstod nyes pa mang sel pa//nor bdun gyis ni 'gro la phan mdzad pa/
23b8(mdo sde): gang gis blo gros zhib dang ldan par gsungs//'gro ba'i mun sel zhi ba'i sgron ma mdzad//shi 'pho na 'chi skye ba mya ngan 'joms//'gro ba ngan 'gro lam las 
24a1(mdo sde): rab bzlog pa//gang gis bdud kyi lam las thar pa gsungs//rigs kyi bu de nas de bzhin gshegs pa 'od zhi spos snang dpal des

24a3(mdo sde): zhe na/'di lta ste/lhag pa'i bsam pas sems can thams cad la snying rje ba dang /ma lta bur sems can thams cad kyi sdug bsngal rab tu zhi bar bya ba la brtson pa 
24a4(mdo sde): dang /chos thams cad la srog med pa dang /gso ba med pa dang /gang zag med pa dang /tha dad du dbyer med cing yongs su rtogs pa ste/skyes bu

24a6(mdo sde): bu dam pa bdud kyi zhags pas mi zin cing bdud kyi lam las thar te/gsum gang zhe na/'di lta ste/sems can thams cad la mi khro zhing klan ka mi tshol ba yin/
24a7(mdo sde): sems can thams cad la mtshungs par sbyin gnas su 'du shes pa yin/chos thams cad la tshul cig tu yongs su rtog ste/'di ltar chos thams cad nam mkha' 
24a8(mdo sde): dang mtshungs shing bcos su med pa tha dad pa ma yin pa ma skyes pa/ma byung ba/ma 'gags pa ste/chos thams cad nam mkha' lta bur rdzas kyi mtshan nyid dang bral bar 
24b1(mdo sde): mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyi rab tu rtog pa'o//rgyal po chen po chos gsum po de dag dang ldan na/skyes bu dam pa bdud kyi zhags pas mi zin cing bdud kyi lam 
24b2(mdo sde): las thar pa yin no//de nas de'i tshe rgyal po chen po ud pa la'i gdong gi btsun mo'i dam pa lha mdzes shes bya ba bud med brgyad khri bzhi stong gis yongs su bskor cing 
24b3(mdo sde): mdun du bdar nas/de bzhin gshegs pa 'od zhi spos snang dpal ga la ba der song ste phyin nas/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa 'od zho spos snang dpal de

24b7(mdo sde): bshad par gsol//bdag gis blo gros thibs po 'dir ni gsal du gsol//'jig rten dag na mi mnyam mnyam pa dge sbyong mchog/rab grags yon tan

25a1(mdo sde): ces gsol ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa 'od zhi spos snang dpal des/btsun mo dam pa lha mdzes la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//
25a2(mdo sde): che zhe rnam grangs gang sems kyi bud med dag bud med kyi dngos po myur du brjes te/sngon 'phangs pa'i bud med kyi dngos po myu du ma lus par zad par 'gyur zhing bla na med pa mya ngan las 
25a3(mdo sde): 'das pa chen po'i bar du rang gi smon lam ma gtogs par bud med du skye ba 'dzin par mi 'gyur ba'i rnam grangs yod do//che zhe de la rnam grangs gang gis bud 
25a4(mdo sde): med myur du skyes par 'gyur zhing /sngon 'phangs pa'i bud me kyi dngos po myur du ma lus par zad par2 gyur ba'i rnam grangs gang zhe na/che zhe de la rin po

25a6(mdo sde): lus dang ngag dang /sems kyi gnas ngan len gyi sdug bsngal rnam par smin pa ma lus par zad par byed do//rin po che tog gi gzungs 'di thos ma thag tu bud 
25a7(mdo sde): med rnams kyi dngos po ma lus par 'gyur te/bud med kyi dbang po mi snang zhing skyes pa'i dbang po skye bar 'gyur/skyes pa'i gzugs bzang po yan lag thams 
25a8(mdo sde): cad tshang bar 'gyur/drang zhing zhib la mkhas par 'gyur/lus dang ngag dang yid kyis legs par byas pa'i las kyi mtha' dang ldan zhing legs par spyod pa sbyang 
25b1(mdo sde): pa dang /dgra thams cad thul bar 'gyur ro//gang gi tshe 'di dang tshe rabs phyi ma lus dang ngag dang /yid kyi sdug pa myong bar 'gyur ba'i nyes par byas pa'i las 
25b2(mdo sde): kyi 'bras bu rnam par smin pa de yongs su zad par 'gyur te/mtshams med pa byed pa dang /dam pa'i chos spong ba dang /'phags pa la bskur pa 'debs pa dag ma 
25b3(mdo sde): gtogs par de dag gi bud med kyi dngos po'i lhag ma lus pa de nyid la yongs su zad par 'gyuro//lus dang ngag dang /yid ki gnas ngan len gyi rnam par smin pa 
25b4(mdo sde): che gzhan la bud med kyi dngos po grub par byed pa'i sa bon sdug bsngal rnam par smin pa myong bar 'gyur ba'i/las kyi sgrib pa'i sa bon gyi rgyu 'thun pa ri rab tsam 
25b5(mdo sde): yang ma lus par yongs su zad par 'gyur ro//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di lta ste/sems can rnams kyi sdug bsngal rnam par sminapa'i las yongs 
25b6(mdo sde): su zad par bya ba dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba rnam par spel ba'i phyir 'das pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyis 
25b7(mdo sde): kyang rin po che tog gi gzungs 'di bshad pa byin gyis brlabaso//gcig la gcig rjes su dgyes so//mngon par bstod do//bsngags so/rab tu bsngags so/
25b8(mdo sde): de ltar phyogs bcu na da ltar byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa dag bcom pa yang dag par rjogs pa'i sangs rgyas gang dag bzhugs te/'tsho zhing bzhes pa'i sangs rgyas

26a3(mdo sde): bcu'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing so sor 'byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas de dag kyang sems can rnams kyi sdug bsngal

317b7(mdo sde): khams gsum na ni nor ba yin//de phyir sna tshogs snang bar 'gyur//'jig rten rmi lam ngo bo nyid mtshungs ste//de ni sna tshogs kyi yang gzugs rnams 
317b8(mdo sde): snang //reg dang longs spyod lus kyi mthar 'gro dang //'jig rten gtso dang byed pa rnams mtshungaso//sems 'di khams gsum skye gnas gyur pa yin//sems
318a1(mdo sde): 'khrul 'di dang pha rol snang ba'o//'jig rton med phyir de la brtag mi bya//'jig rten de 'dra'i rang bzhin rtogs nas ni//mi gtsang gnas 
318a2(mdo sde): kyi mtha' mar phyin par 'gyur//byis pa rnams ni gti mug phyir//'byung ba dang ni 'jig pa mthong //shes rab ldan pa rnam dag ni//'byung dang 'jig pa mthong 
318a3(mdo sde): ba med//lha yi pho brang 'og min po//sdig pa thams cad rnam par spangs//rtag tu rnam par mi rtog ldan//sems dang sems las byung ba spangs//stobs dang 
318a4(mdo sde): mngon shes dbang thob pa//de dang ting 'dzin dbang rnyed nas//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas der sangs rgyas//sprul pa rnams ni 'dir 'tshang rgya//sangs rgyas rnams ni 
318a5(mdo sde): sprul pa dag/bye ba dpag med 'byung bar 'gyur//byis pa kun na de dag las//brag cha 'dra chos bsam ya sa nyan//thog ma dbus dang mtha' las 
318a6(mdo sde): grol//dngos po dang ni dngos med spangs//khyab pa mi g.yo dag pa ste//sna tshogs med las sna tshogs 'byung //rnam par rig pa'i rigs kyi g.yogs//lus can kun las 
318a7(mdo sde): gnas pa ste//sgyu ma nor ba yod mod kyi//rgyu ma nor ba'i rgyu ma yin//'jig rten dag na sems bslu ba//cung zad tsam ni gang yod pa//rang bzhin gnyis dang bral ba ste//
318a8(mdo sde): kun gzhi rnam par shes pas sprul//rnam rig tsam gyi lta shwa dang //gang zag chos su spyod par yang //'jig rten de ltar rnam bshig nas//nam zhig 
318b1(mdo sde): shin tu gyur pa na//de tshe nga yi bu yin te//rdzogs pa'i chos rnams rab ston pa//dro dang gsher dang sra g.yo bar//chos rnams byis pas nor par btags//yang dag
318b2(mdo sde): ma yin sgro btags te//mtshan nyid med cing mtshan gzhi med//rgyun rnams kyi ni lus 'di la//'di ni rdzas brgyad gzugs yin te//lus kyi dbyibs dang dbang por ni//
318b3(mdo sde): byis pa nor pa rnam rtog go/rgyu dang rkyen rnams tshogs pa las//byis ba 'byung bar rtog par byed//tshul 'di dag ni mi shes pas//srid pa gsum gyi gnas
318b4(mdo sde): na 'khyam//dngos po thams cad rang bzhin med pa ste//rtog pa ma yi tshig yin de yang med//srid pa rmi lam sprul bzhin brtag byas nas//'khor bar
318b5(mdo sde): mi bya mya ngan 'da' mi bya//sna tshogs sa bon zhes ni bya ba'i sems//sems kyi spyod yul snang ba ste//snang nas rtog pa gnyis dag ste//byis pa rnams ni skye
318b6(mdo sde): bar rtogs//mi shes pa dang sred dang las//sems dang sems las byung gnas te//gang phyir de las byung bas na//de ni gzhan gyi dbang du 'dod//de dag gang dngos
318b7(mdo sde): rtogs pa'i ni//sems kyi spyod yul nor pa ste//rtogs pas grub pa ma yin no//log pa nor pa rnam par brtags//rten rnams dang ni 'brel pa las//lus
318b8(mdo sde): can rnams kyi sems 'byung ngo//rkyen rnams las ni rnam grol zhing //rang gi mtshan nyid rnam spangs te//nam zhig lus la mi gnas na//de phyir nga yi
319a1(mdo sde): @#//spyod yul min//ji ltar rgyal po tshong dpon dang //ri dgas 'dra ba sna tshogs kyis//bu brid khyim na rtser bcug nas//nags kyi ri dgas phrad pa bstan//
319a2(mdo sde): de ltar ngas ni chos rnams kyi//gzugs brnyan mtshan nyid sna tshogs kyis//so so rang rig yang dag mtha'//bu rnams la ni nga 'chad do//ji ltar rgya mtsho'i rlabs
319a3(mdo sde): rnams ni//rlung gi rkyen gyis byung ba dag//gar byed rab tu 'jug par 'gyur//rgyun kyang chad par mi 'gyur ro//de bzhin kun gzhi chu bo yang //rtag tu
319a4(mdo sde): yul gyi rkyen gyis byung //rnam shes dba' rlabs sna tshogs kyis//gar byed rab tu 'dud par 'gyur//gzung dang 'dzin pa'i dngos por ni//lus 
319a5(mdo sde): can rnams kyi sems 'jug go/ji ltar byis pas btags pa ltar//snang ba yi ni mtshan nyid med//kun gzhi rnam shes dam pa ni//rnam par rig ste kun gzhi 
319a6(mdo sde): yang //gzung dang 'dzin pa med pa'i phyir//de bzhin nyid du ngas bshad do//phung po rnams la bdag med de//srog kyang med cing gang zag med//rnam par shes ni skye bar 'gyur//
319a7(mdo sde): rnam par shes ni 'gags par 'gyur//ji ltar ri mo mthon dman du//snang yang yod pa ma yin pa'i//de bzhin dngos po rnams la yang //dngos nyid snang yang yod pa min//
319a8(mdo sde): ji ltar dri za'i grong khyer dang //ji lta bur ni smig rgyu dang //de ltar snang ba rtog mthong yang //shes rab kyis ni yod pa min//tshad ma dbang po rnam par log/'bras bu med cing rgyu yang 
319b1(mdo sde): med||blo dang rig par bya dang bral//mtshan dang mtshan gyi gzhi yang spangs//phung por rten cing sangs rgyas ba//gang na'ang sus kyang ma mthong ste//gang na sus kyang ma mthong ba//de la
319b2(mdo sde): ji ltar rnam par bsgom//rkyen dang gtan tshigs dpe rnams dang //dam bca' ba dang rgyu rnams kyis//rmi lam dri za'i 'khor lo dang //smig rgyu nyi ma zla ba dang //myu gu 'od 
319b3(mdo sde): sogs dpe rnams kyi//skye ba de ni ngas bzlog go/'gro ba rmi lam sgyu ma 'dra//stong pa dang ni brtags pas ste//'jig rten gsum la mi gnas shing //phyi nang du'ang 
319b4(mdo sde): de bzhin te//srid pa ma skyes mthong bas na//mi skye bzod pa 'byung bar 'gyur//sgyu ma lta bu'i ting 'dzin dang //lus kyang sgyu ma lta bu dang //mngon shes dbang dang
319b5(mdo sde): stobs rnams kyang //de yi sems la sna tshogs 'gyur//gang gis dngos po ma skyes pa//stong pa shin tu rang bzhin med//de dag la ni rkyen rnams kyis//nor pa skye 
319b6(mdo sde): zhing 'gags par 'gyur//phyi rol snang ba'i gzugs can gyis//sems dang sems ni snang bar 'gyur//ji ltar byis pas btags pa ltar//mthong bar bya ba gzhan medo//'brel ba rnams
319b7(mdo sde): dang sangs rgyas gzugs//'byung ba rnams ni 'jig pa dang //shin tu bslabs pas rnam rig gis//'gro ba sna tshogs byin gyis rlob//lus dang gnas dang longs
319b8(mdo sde): spyod rnams//gzung ba rnam rig gsum po ste//yid dang 'dzin gyi rnam rig dang //rnam par rtog ni 'dzin pa gsum//rnam par rtog dang 
320a1(mdo sde): @#//rnam brtag pa//ji srid yi ge'i spyod yul ba//rtog can rtog ges nos pa'i phyir//de srid yang dag mi thong ngo//gang gi tshe na shes rab kyis//
320a2(mdo sde): dngos po rang bzhin med par shes//de yi tshe na rnal 'byor can//mtshan nyid gnas shing ngal yang bso//bya gag snag tshas bskus pa la//de nyid 'dri bar mi shes 'dzin//
320a3(mdo sde): mi shes pa yi byis pa dag/theg pa gsum la'ang de bzhin no//'di la nyan thos gang yang med//rang rgyal ba dag de bzhin te//nyan thos dang ni rang rgyal ba'i//

320a5(mdo sde): btags pa ste//chos dang gang zag 'phos pa'i phyir//de gyur pa ni de bzhin nyid//nyi ma zla ba mar me 'od//de bzhin 'byung ba nor bu rnams//rnam par mi rtog
320a6(mdo sde): shin tu 'byung //de bzhin sangs rgyas sangs rgyas nyid//ji ltar rab rib can dag gis//skra shad log par 'dzings pa ltar//de bzhin dngos por rtog pa 'di//byis 
320a7(mdo sde): pas log par rnam par btags//nyon mongs rnam byang dngos po rnams//skra shad 'dzing ba lta bu ste//gnas dang 'jig dang skye dang bral//rtag dang mi rtag 
320a8(mdo sde): spangs pa'o//ji ltar la las d+hA tur zos//gser lta bur ni 'gro ba mthong //de na gser yod ma yin yang //sa dag kyang ni gser du snang//
320b1(mdo sde): thogs pa med sems sems 'byung bas//de ltar byis pa nyams bar byas//srid pa sgyu ma smig sgyu skyes//yang dag nyid du 'dzin par byed//sa bon gcig dang

320b4(mdo sde): bya'o//'di la rkyen gyis mi skye ste//gang yang 'gag par mi 'gyur ro//btags pa yi ni rkyen rnams nyid//skye ba dang ni 'gag par 'gyur//srid pa gsum ni btags

320b7(mdo sde): de phyir sgyu ma lta bur rig/bskyed pa med cing skye ba med//rkyen rnams kyang ni gang na yang //gar yang yod pa ma yin te//kha cig gis ni spyod par brjod//rkyen gyi skye 'jig

321a1(mdo sde): @#//rig med //kun gzhi med cing dngos med na//byis pa ngan pa'i rtog ge pa//ro dang 'dra bas 'di dag brtags//nam zhig 'jig rten sems tsam du //
321a2(mdo sde): rgyal ba'i sras kyis mthong bas na//de tshe rigasu gtogs pa'i lus//bya ba'i 'du byed spangs pa ste//stobs dang mngon par shes pa dang //dbang dang ldan par rab tu 'thob//
321a3(mdo sde): thams cad gzugasu snang ba yi//sems de nam zhig byung ba na//'di la sems med gzugs mod kyi//nor sems thog ma med ces pa//de tshe rnal 'byor can

321a6(mdo sde): po thams cad ma skyes te//nor pa tsam ni snang bar 'gyur//byis pa rtog gnyis dga' ba dag/nor pa la ni skyes par rtog/sna tshogs bag chags 'byung ba 
321a7(mdo sde): yi//skye ba'i char ni gtogs pa'i sems//chu bo rlabs bzhin shin tu 'byung //de chad nas ni mi 'byung ngo //rnam pa sna tshogs pa'i sems//sna tshogs la ni 'jug pa

321b1(mdo sde): na//sems kyang rkyen gyis bskyed 'gyur te//sems tsam du ni mi rung ngo//sems kyis su ni sems las gzung //rgyu dang bcas pa gang yang med//sems kyi chos nyid

322a1(mdo sde): kyi mchog/'og min shin tu rnam par mdzes//mi yi ji lta de bzhin du//de yi 'od zer sna tshogs pa//yid 'ong zhi ba rab tu 'byung //de dag

322a7(mdo sde): lus par//sems tsam la ni yod pa yin//rgyun du zhugs pa'i 'bras bu dang //lan cig phyir 'ong 'bras bu dang //phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu dang //dgra bcom nyid 
322a8(mdo sde): sems nor pa'o//byis pa rnams ni 'dus byas la//mi rtag skad cig stong par rtog/sa bon 'bab chu me mar dpes//skad cig pa'i don du rtog/
322b1(mdo sde): byed pa med cing skad cig pa //dben pa bya ba spangs pa dang //chos rnams kyi ni mi skye ba//skad cig don du ngas bshad do//yod dang med la skye bar ni//khyad par pa dang grang+sa
322b2(mdo sde): can shes//lung du ma bstan thams cad kyang //de dag nyid kyis bshad pa'o//lung bstan pa ni rnam bzhi ste//mgo cig dang ni dri ba dang //rnam par 'byed dang

322b4(mdo sde): pa'i don du 'dod na ni//dmigs pa rang bzhin med pa la/de phyir kun rdzob bstan pa yin//dngos po brjod pa'i rgyus byung ba//gal te rang bzhin yod na ni//
322b5(mdo sde): brjod pa dag las byung ba yin//dngos po med ces bya ba med//brjod pa 'di ni dngos med de//kun rdzob du yang yod ma yin//phyin ci log ni bzhi yin bas
322b6(mdo sde): dmigs pa yod pa ma yin no//gal te phyin ci log yod na//ngo bo nyid med med par gyur//phyin ci log ni bzhi yi phyir//gang dang gang dag dmigs 'gyur ba//

323a1(mdo sde): rtog pa yis//stong pa nyid ni yang dag mthong //sgyu ma glang po sna tshogs ltar//lo ma rnams dang gser bzhin du//mi shes pas ni sems

324b4(mdo sde): de dag brjod du med//ngo bo nyid kyang med par bshad//rkyen gyis bskyed pa'i don la ni//yod dang med pa yod ma yin//rkyen du gtogs pa'i dngos po la//yod

324b6(mdo sde): phan tshun rnam par rtog pa yis//goms shing goms par byed pa'o//ming ni brjod par mi byas na//'jig rten thams cad smongs par 'gyur//de bas rmongs pa bsal ba'i
324b7(mdo sde): phyir||ming du brtags pa bya ba yin//ming dang nor pa'i rnam rtog dang//rkyen rnams kyis ni bskyed pa yi//rnam par rtog pa gsum dag dag gis//byis pa rnams ni dngos por rtog//
324b8(mdo sde): 'gegs pa med cing ma skyes te//rang bzhin gyis ni nam mkha' 'dra//dngos po med pa'i rang bzhin gang //de dag rnams btags mtshan nyid do//mig yor 'dra zhing sgyu ma bzhin//
325a1(mdo sde): @#//smig sgyu rmi lam mgal me bskor//dri za'i grong khyer brag cha 'dra//chos de rkyen las byung ba yin//de bzhin nyid dang gnyis su med//stong pa chos nyid
325a2(mdo sde): yang dag mtha'//rnam par mi rtog bshad pa ni//gang de grub pa'i mtshan nyid do//lus dang ngag gi spyod yul rdzun//shes rab mi rtog bden pa ste//sems ni gnyis kyi
325a3(mdo sde): mthar lhung bas//de phyir shes rab rnam mi rtog//ji srid sems kyi spyod yul la//yod dang med pa'i mtha' gnyis yin//spyod yul spangs par gyur na ni//de
325a4(mdo sde): tshe sems kyang yang dag 'gag/rkyen las skye ba'i rang bzhin la//rtog pa gang gis rtog byed pa//phyi rol don mthong log pa ste//don med sems ni

325a6(mdo sde): chags kyis ni dkrugs pa'i sems//don du snang ba shin tu 'byung //rtog pa'i rnam pa gnyis 'gags na//yang dag spyod yul ye shes te//'phags pa'i spyod yul
325a7(mdo sde): bsam mi khyab//snang ba med par 'byung bar 'gyur//ming dang mtshan ma kun rtog pa//rang bzhin gnyis kyi mtshan nyid do//yang dag ye shes de bzhin nyid yongs
325a8(mdo sde): su grub pa'i mtshan nyid do//pha ma phrad par 'gyur ba'i phyir//kun gzhi yod dang ldan par ni//mar gyi bum pa'i byi ba bzhin//khu chu dang ni lhan cig
325b1(mdo sde): skyes//sla gar sman du 'bras ma sdug/las kyis rnam pa sna tshogs byas//las rlung 'byung ba chen po yis//shing tog bzhin du smin par 'gyur//lnga dang lnga dang 
325b2(mdo sde): lnga nyid dang //rma yang dgu po nyid rnams dang //so dang se mo spus g.yogs nas//'gul zhing rab tu skye bas 'gyur//mi gtsang srin bu skyes ma thag/mi dag nyal

325b5(mdo sde): bsam bzhin bzhugs//ngan par lta ba'i gnas med de//so so rang rig theg pa ni//rtog ge ba yi spyod yul min//mgon po 'das pa'i 'og tu ni//'dzin par 
325b6(mdo sde): su 'gyur bshad du bsol//bder gshegs mya ngan 'das pa'i 'og/tshul rnams 'dzin par 'gyur ba de//dus ngag 'das nas 'byung ba ni//blo gros chen po 
325b7(mdo sde): khyod shes byos//lho phyogs be de'i yul du ni//dge slong dpal ldan cher grags pa//de ming klu zhes bod pa ste//yod dang med pa'i phyogs 'jigs pa//
325b8(mdo sde): nga yi theg pa 'jig rten du//bla med theg chen rab bshad nas//rab tu dga' ba'i sa bsgrubs te//bde ba can du de 'gro'o//'gro ba kun tu rab 'khor bas//
326a1(mdo sde): @#//srid pa las ni skyo ba'i yid//zhi ba zhi lam song nas ni//rnal 'byor sna tshogs dam la rtsom//rnal 'byor can gyis brtson byas na//nyi ma zla ba'i
326a2(mdo sde): dbyibs 'dra dang //'od dang pad mo lta bu dang //ri mo nam mkha'i me 'dra mthong //mtshan ma sna tshogs de dag ni//mu stegs can lam khrid par byed//
326a3(mdo sde): nyan thos dag dang rang rgyal gyi//spyod yul tu 'ang ltung bar byed//yul la 'dzin pa med pa'i phyir//'gog pa med pa ma yin te//'phags pa rnams kyi spyod
326a4(mdo sde): yul ltar//de bzhin nyid kyi dngos por yod//mkhas pa rnams la snang ba ltar//byis pa rnams la mi snang ste//ji ltar chos kun ma skyes shing //mtshan 
326a5(mdo sde): nyid med par snang ba'o//gser min gser ltar snang ba yi//ra gan spungs la byis pa rnams//gser du ji ltar rtog pa bzhin//ngan pa'i rtog ges chos

326a8(mdo sde): rten cing 'brel 'byung bar//rgyal ba dag ni mi gsung gi//'jig rten 'di ni rkyen nyid de//dri za yi ni grong khyer 'dra//'di yod na ni 'di zhes bya//

326b2(mdo sde): de dag la ni gzugs brnyan snang //gzugs brnyan gang na'ang yod pa min//ji ltar namakha'i smig sgyu ba//dngos por snang sems de bzhin te//rmi lam mo sham
326b3(mdo sde): bu bzhin du//rnam pa sna tshogs gzugs su snang //theg chen nga yi theg pa min//yi ge med cing sgra yang med//bden pa ma yin thar pa min//snang med spyod
326b4(mdo sde): yul ma yin no//'on kyang theg chen theg pa ste//ting 'dzin la yang dbang byed pa//yid kyi lus ni sna tshogs pa//dbang gi me tog rnams kyis brgyan//

326b6(mdo sde): skyes pa'i stong pa nyid kyang cig//ma skyes stong pa mchog yin te//skyes pa'i stong pa 'jig par 'gyur//stong pa de bzhin nyid dang mtha'//mya ngan 'das dang chos

327a7(mdo sde): de bzhin snang la byis ba rnams//'phags pa rnams ni bye brag tu//'phags pa'i lta ba dag pa ste//rnam par thar pa gsum las byung //rang dang spyi yi
327a8(mdo sde): mtshan nyid rnams//nyan thos rnams la rab tu bshad//thams cad chos la ma chags dang //cig pu dben par spyod pa dang //ma brtags pa yi 'bras bu
327b1(mdo sde): ni rang rgyal sras po dag la bshad//phyi rol btags pa'i rang bzhin te/gzhin dbang rang gi bzhi la skyes//bdag las skyes pa'i 'khrul ma mthong //de phyir sems
